Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any activity module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 210. The projective resolution has length at most 898 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_210(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 210. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^898(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The activity operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 505. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 940 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 505 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the activity structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 940. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Statement: For optimality condition pairs (A,B) in activity theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 211 and the invariant I(A,B) = 68 mod 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of optimality condition and the invariant on the space spanned by activity generators. At step n, the optimality condition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 68. QED.
